SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS
The Group aims to establish fair and transparent procedures to enable all shareholders an equal opportunity to exercise
their rights in an informed manner and communicate efficiently with the Group. Under the Articles of Association and
the relevant policies and procedures of the Group, the shareholders have, among others, the following rights:

Convene an Extraordinary General Meeting
General meetings shall be convened on the written requisition of any two or more members deposited at the principal
office of the Company in Hong Kong (3/F, Crystal Industrial Building, 71 How Ming Street, Kwun Tong, Kowloon, Hong
Kong) or, in the event the Company ceases to have a principal office, the registered office specifying the objects of the
meeting and signed by the requisitionists, provided that such requisitionists held at the date of deposit of the requisition
not less than one-tenth of the paid up capital of the Company carrying the right to vote at general meetings of the
Company. General meetings may also be convened on the written requisition of any one member which is a recognised
clearing house (or its nominee(s)) deposited at the principal office of the Company in Hong Kong (3/F, Crystal Industrial
Building, 71 How Ming Street, Kwun Tong, Kowloon, Hong Kong) or, in the event the Company ceases to have a
principal office, the registered office specifying the objects of the meeting and signed by the requisitionist, provided that
such requisitionist held at the date of deposit of the requisition not less than one-tenth of the paid up capital of the
Company carrying the right to vote at general meetings of the Company. If the Board does not within 21 days from the
date of deposit of the requisition proceed duly to convene the meeting to be held within a further 21 days, the
requisitionist(s) themselves or any of them representing more than one-half of the total voting rights of all of them, may
convene the general meeting in the same manner, as nearly as possible, as that in which meetings may be convened by
the Board provided that any meeting so convened shall not be held after the expiration of three months from the date of
deposit of the requisition, and all reasonable expenses incurred by the requisitionist(s) as a result of the failure of the
Board shall be reimbursed to them by the Company. The Company will take appropriate action and make all necessary
arrangements in accordance with the requirements of Article 12.3 of the Company’s Articles of Association once a valid
requisition is received.

Propose a Person for Election as a Director
The Company has adopted the procedures for shareholders to propose a person for election as a director with effect
from the Listing Date. The procedures are available on the Group’s website.

     1. GENERAL                                                                 1. 一般資料
         The Company was previously incorporated in Bermuda as an                本公司先前在百慕達註冊成立為獲豁免
         exempted company with limited liability. Pursuant to a special          有限責任公司。根據過往年度通過的一
         resolution passed in prior year, the Company discontinued in            項特別決議案，本公司取消在百慕達註
         Bermuda and registered by way of continuation in the Cayman             冊並以存續方式於開曼群島註冊為獲豁
         Islands as an exempted company with limited liability. Its parent       免有限責任公司。其母公司及最終控股
         and ultimate holding company is Crystal Group Limited                   公司為 Crystal Group Limited（先前在百
         (previously incorporated in Bermuda and redomiciled in the              慕達註冊成立後遷冊至開曼群島）。本公
         Cayman Islands). The address of the registered office of the            司 的 註 冊 辦 事 處 地 址 為 Ugland House,
         Company is Ugland House, P.O. Box 309, Grand Cayman KY1-                P.O. Box 309, Grand Cayman KY1-1104,
         1104, Cayman Islands and the principal place of business of the         Cayman Islands，而本公司的主要營業
         Company is 3/F, Crystal Industrial Building, 71 How Ming Street,        地點為香港觀塘巧明街 71 號晶苑工業大
         Kwun Tong, Hong Kong.                                                   廈三樓。

         The shares of the Company were listed on the Main Board of               本公司股份於二零一七年十一月三日
         The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                     （「上市日期」）在香港聯合交易所有限公

   lessors and lessees. IFRS 16 will supersede IAS 17 “Leases”               個綜合模式。當國際財務報告準則第 16

                                                                            「租賃」及相關詮釋。

   IFRS 16 distinguishes lease and service contracts on the basis of        國際財務報告準則第 16 號根據所識別資
   whether an identified asset is controlled by a customer.                 產是否由客戶控制區分租賃及服務合約。

   for lessee accounting, and are replaced by a model where a right-        賃及融資租賃的差異在承租人會計處理

   of low value assets.

   less accumulated depreciation and impairment losses, adjusted            值虧損計量，就任何重新計量租賃負債
   for any remeasurement of the lease liability. The lease liability is     作出調整。租賃負債初步按當日尚未支
   initially measured at the present value of the lease payments that       付租賃付款的現值計量。其後，租賃負
   are not paid at that date. Subsequently, the lease liability is          債就（其中包括）利息及租賃付款以及租
   adjusted for interest and lease payments, as well as the impact          賃修訂的影響作出調整。就現金流量分
   of lease modifications, amongst others. For the classification of        類而言，本集團現時就自用租賃土地將
   cash flows, the Group currently presents upfront prepaid lease           預付租賃付款呈列為投資現金流量，而
   payments as investing cash flows in relation to leasehold lands          其他經營租賃付款則呈列為經營現金流
   for own use while other operating lease payments are presented           量。根據國際財務報告準則第 16 號，有
   as operating cash flows. Under IFRS 16, lease payments in                關租賃負債的租賃付款將於本金及利息

     3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                           3. 重要會計政策
         The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in               綜合財務報表乃根據由國際會計準則理
         accordance with IFRSs issued by the IASB. In addition, the                事會頒佈的國際財務報告準則編製。此
         consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures          外，綜合財務報表包括聯交所證券上市
         required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the          規則及香港公司條例規定的適用披露。
         Stock Exchange and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

         The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on the           除若干物業及金融工具按於各報告期末
         historical cost basis except for certain properties and financial         的重估金額或公平值計量外，綜合財務
         instruments that are measured at revalued amounts or fair values          報表乃根據歷史成本基準編製而成，按
         at the end of each reporting period, as explained in the                  下文所載的會計政策所闡釋。
         accounting policies set out below.

         Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the               歷史成本一般根據就交換貨品及服務而
         consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                   付出的代價公平值計算。

         Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or         公平值為於計量日期市場參與者於有序
         paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between             交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負債所支
         market participants at the measurement date, regardless of                 付的價格，而不論該價格是直接觀察所
         whether that price is directly observable or estimated using               得抑或運用另一估值技術估計得出。在
         another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value of an            估計資產或負債的公平值時，本集團會
         asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the                     考慮市場參與者於計量日期對資產或負
         characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants would     債定價時所考慮的資產或負債的特徵。
         take those characteristics into account when pricing the asset or          在綜合財務報表中計量及╱或披露的公
         liability at the measurement date. Fair value for measurement              平值均在此基礎上予以釐定，但國際財
         and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated financial                 務報告準則第2號「以股份為基礎的付款」
         statements is determined on such a basis, except for share-                範圍內的以股份為基礎付款交易、國際
         based payment transactions that are within the scope of IFRS 2             會計準則第 17 號範圍內的租賃交易以及
         “Share-based Payment”, leasing transactions that are within the            與公平值有若干類似之處但並非公平值
         scope of IAS 17, and measurements that have some similarities              的計量（例如國際會計準則第 2 號「存貨」
         to fair value but are not fair value, such as net realisable value in      中的可變現淨值或國際會計準則第 36 號
         IAS 2 “Inventories” or value in use in IAS 36 “Impairment of              「資產減值」中的使用價值）除外。
         Assets”.

         A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into              非金融資產的公平值計量參考市場參與
         account a market participant’s ability to generate economic               者可從使用該資產得到的最高及最佳效
         benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by             用，或將該資產售予另一可從使用該資
         selling it to another market participant that would use the asset         產得到最高及最佳效用的市場參與者以
         in its highest and best use.                                              產生經濟效益的能力。

   before the end of that reporting period. If the recoverable amount      單位或現金產生單位組別會於該報告期

     3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                           3. 重要會計政策（續）
         (Continued)
         Investment in an associate                                                於聯營公司的投資
         An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant             聯營公司乃本集團對其有重大影響的實
         influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the        體。重大影響乃參與投資對象的財務及
         financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but is not        經營決策的權力，但並非對該等政策具
         control or joint control over those policies.                              有控制權或共同控制權。

         The results and assets and liabilities of an associate are                 聯營公司的業績、資產及負債乃以會計
         incorporated in these consolidated financial statements using the          權益法計入該等綜合財務報表。就權益
         equity method of accounting. The financial statements of an                會計而言，聯營公司財務報表的編製使
         associate used for equity accounting purposes are prepared                 用本集團於類似情況下就相近交易及事
         using accounting policies that are uniform with those of the               件的統一會計政策。根據權益法，於聯
         Group for like transactions and events in similar circumstances.           營公司的投資乃按成本於綜合財務狀況
         Under the equity method, investment in an associate is initially           表中首次確認，其後就確認本集團分佔
         recognised in the consolidated statement of financial position at          該聯營公司的損益及其他全面收入作出
         cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of             調整。於聯營公司資產淨值（損益及其他
         the profit or loss and other comprehensive income of the                   全面收入除外）的變動並無入賬，除非有
         associate. Changes in net assets of the associate other than               關變動導致本集團所持擁有權權益有
         profit or loss and other comprehensive income are not accounted            變。當本集團分佔聯營公司的虧損等於
         for unless such changes resulted in changes in ownership                   或超出本集團於該聯營公司的權益（包括
         interest held by the Group. When the Group’s share of losses of            任何長期權益，而該長期權益實質上構
         an associate equals or exceeds its interest in that associate              成本集團於該聯營公司的投資淨額的一
         (which includes any long-term interests that, in substance, form           部分），則本集團不再繼續確認其分佔的
         part of the Group’s net investment in the associate), the Group            進一步虧損。額外虧損僅以本集團已產
         discontinues recognising its share of further losses. Additional           生法定或推定責任或代表該聯營公司付
         losses are recognised only to the extent that the Group has                款者為限而確認。
         incurred legal or constructive obligations or made payments on
         behalf of the associate.

         An investment in an associate is accounted for using the equity            於投資對象成為聯營公司當日起，於聯
         method from the date on which the investee becomes an                      營公司的投資採用權益法入賬。於收購
         associate. On acquisition of the investment in an associate, any           一間聯營公司的投資時，投資成本超過
         excess of the cost of the investment over the Group’s share of             本集團分佔該投資對象可識別資產及負
         the net fair value of the identifiable assets and liabilities of the       債公平淨值的任何部分乃確認為商譽，
         investee is recognised as goodwill, which is included within the           並計入投資的賬面值。於重新評估後，
         carrying amount of the investment. Any excess of the Group’s               本集團分佔可識別資產及負債公平淨值
         share of the net fair value of the identifiable assets and liabilities     超過投資成本的任何部分，會於收購投
         over the cost of the investment, after reassessment, is                    資期間即時於損益確認。
         recognised immediately in profit or loss in the period in which
         the investment is acquired.

         Revenue from contracts with customers (upon                               客戶合約收益（應用國際財務報
         application of IFRS 15)                                                   告準則第 15 號後）
         Under IFRS 15, the Group recognises revenue when (or as) a                 根據國際財務報告準則第 15 號，本集團
         performance obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the            於履行履約責任時（或就此）確認收益，
         goods or services underlying the particular performance                    即與特定履約責任相關的貨品或服務的
         obligation is transferred to the customer.                                「控制權」轉移予客戶時。

         A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a                履約責任指某項特定貨品或服務（或一批
         bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct      貨品或服務）或大致上相同的一系列特定
         goods or services that are substantially the same.                         貨品或服務。

         Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over            倘符合以下其中一項條件，控制權於一
         time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction            段時間內轉移，而收益則參考履行相關
         of the relevant performance obligation if one of the following             履約責任的進度於一段時間內確認：
         criteria is met:

         •     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the                •   客戶於本集團履約時同時接收及使
               benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the Group                用本集團履約帶來的利益；
               performs;

         •     the Group’s performance creates and enhances an asset                •   本集團履約創造及加強客戶隨著本
               that the customer controls as the Group performs; or                     集團履約而控制的資產；或

         •     the Group’s performance does not create an asset with an             •   本集團履約並未創造對本集團而言
               alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                        另有用途的資產，而本集團享有可
               enforceable right to payment for performance completed to                強制執行的權利就迄今所履行的履
               date.                                                                    約獲得付款。

         Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the               除此之外，收益於客戶取得特定貨品或
         customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.                  服務控制權的時間點確認。

   from the customer.

   unless it is clear that both elements are operating leases in which     情況下整項租賃分類為經營租賃。具體
   case the entire lease is classified as an operating lease.              而言，最低租賃款項（包括任何一次性預
   Specifically, the minimum lease payments (including any lump-           付款）在租賃期開始時，按租賃土地部分

   The recoverable amount of tangible and intangible assets are              有形及無形資產的可收回金額乃個別地
   estimated individually, when it is not possible to estimate the           進行估計，倘無法估計個別資產的可收
   recoverable amount of an individual asset, the Group estimates            回金額，則本集團會估計該資產所屬現
   the recoverable amount of the CGU to which the asset belongs.             金產生單位的可收回金額。倘可識別合
   When a reasonable and consistent basis of allocation can be               理及一致的分配基準，則企業資產亦會
   identified, corporate assets are also allocated to individual CGUs,       分配至個別現金產生單位，或分配至可
   or otherwise they are allocated to the smallest group of CGUs for         按合理及一致分配基準識別的最小組別
   which a reasonable and consistent allocation basis can be                 現金產生單位。
   identified.

   Intangible assets with indefinite useful lives and intangible assets      具無限可使用年期的無形資產及尚未可
   not yet available for use are tested for impairment at least              供使用的無形資產乃至少每年及每當有
   annually, and whenever there is an indication that they may be            跡象顯示可能減值時進行減值測試。
   impaired.

   The recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs of          可收回金額為公平值減出售成本與使用
   disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the                 價值兩者中的較高值。於評估使用價值
   estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present value         時，乃以反映目前市場對金錢時間價值
   using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market                及估計未來現金流量未調整的資產（或現
   assessments of the time value of money and the risks specific to          金產生單位）的獨有風險的稅前貼現率將
   the asset (or a CGU) for which the estimates of future cash flows         估計未來現金流量貼現至現值。
   have not been adjusted.

   If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a CGU) is estimated to          倘估計資產（或現金產生單位）的可收回
   be less than its carrying amount, the carrying amount of the              金額少於其賬面值，資產（或現金產生單
   asset (or CGU) is reduced to its recoverable amount. In allocating        位）的賬面值下調至其可收回金額。於分
   the impairment loss, the impairment loss is allocated first to            配減值虧損時，首先分配減值虧損以減
   reduce the carrying amount of any goodwill (if applicable) and            少任何商譽的賬面值（如適用），然後按
   then to the other assets on a pro-rata basis based on the carrying        比例根據該單位各資產的賬面值分配至
   amount of each asset in the unit. The carrying amount of an               其他資產。資產賬面值不得減至低於其
   asset is not reduced below the highest of its fair value less costs       公平值減出售成本（如可計量）、其使用
   of disposal (if measurable), its value in use (if determinable) and       價值（如可計量）及零三者中的最高值。
   zero. The amount of the impairment loss that would otherwise              原可另行分配至資產的減值虧損金額按
   have been allocated to the asset is allocated pro rata to the other       比例分配至該單位其他資產。減值虧損
   assets of the unit. An impairment loss is recognised immediately          即時於損益確認，除非相關資產根據另
   in profit or loss, unless the relevant asset is carried at a revalued     一準則按重估值入賬，在此情況下減值
   amount under another standard, in which case the impairment               虧損根據該準則以重估值減少處理。
   loss is treated as a revaluation decrease under that standard.

          Taxation                                                                 稅項
          Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                所得稅開支指現時應付稅項及遞延稅項
          payable and deferred tax.                                                 的總和。

          The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.        現時應付稅項按年內應課稅溢利計算。
          Taxable profit differs from “profit before tax” as reported in the        應課稅溢利與綜合損益及其他全面收入
          consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive          表所報「除稅前溢利」不同，此乃由於在
          income because of items of income or expense that are taxable             其他年度應課稅或可扣減的收支項目從
          or deductible in other years and items that are never taxable or          未課稅或扣稅。本集團即期稅項負債採
          deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using     用於報告期末已實施或大致上已實施的
          tax rates that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the          稅率計量。
          end of the reporting period.

          Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between               遞延稅項乃按綜合財務報表內資產及負
          the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated        債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相應
          financial statements and the corresponding tax bases used in the          稅基的暫時性差額確認。遞延稅項負債
          computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally     一般就所有應課稅暫時性差額確認。遞
          recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax            延稅項資產一般就所有可扣減暫時性差
          assets are generally recognised for all deductible temporary              額確認，惟遞延稅項資產只限於在有應
          differences to the extent that it is probable that taxable profits        課稅溢利可供對銷可扣減暫時性差額時
          will be available against which those deductible temporary                方予以確認。如暫時性差額由商譽或由
          differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax assets and                 初始確認（業務合併除外）一項不影響應
          liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference arises         課稅溢利或會計溢利的交易的其他資產
          from goodwill or from the initial recognition (other than in a            及負債所產生，有關遞延稅項資產及負
          business combination) of other assets and liabilities in a                債不予確認。此外，如初始確認商譽產
          transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the               生暫時性差額，則不予確認遞延稅項負
          accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not          債。
          recognised if the temporary difference arises from the initial
          recognition of goodwill.

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                        3. 重要會計政策（續）
   (Continued)
   Taxation (Continued)                                                    稅項（續）
   Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary            遞延稅項負債乃按因於附屬公司的投資
   differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and              與於一間聯營公司的權益引致的應課稅
   interest in an associate, except where the Group is able to              暫時性差額而確認，惟倘本集團可控制
   control the reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable      暫時性差額的撥回及暫時性差額很可能
   that the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable        於可見將來無法撥回的情況除外。與該
   future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary            等投資相關的可扣稅暫時性差額所產生
   differences associated with such investments and interest are            的遞延稅項資產，僅於很可能有足夠應
   only recognised to the extent that it is probable that there will be     課稅溢利可抵銷暫時性差額且預計於可
   sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of      見將來可以撥回時確認。
   the temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in
   the foreseeable future.

   The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the            遞延稅項資產的賬面值於報告期末進行
   end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is no      審閱，並於不再有足夠應課稅溢利可用
   longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be available to     以收回所有或部分資產時作出相應扣減。
   allow all or part of the assets to be recovered.

   Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates        遞延稅項資產和負債，以報告期末已頒
   that are expected to apply in the period in which the liability is       佈或實質上已頒佈的稅率（和稅法）為基
   settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rates (and tax laws)      礎，按預期清償該負債或變現該資產當
   that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of            期的稅率計量。
   the reporting period.

   The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects          遞延稅項負債和資產的計量反映本集團
   the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                在報告期末預期收回或清償其資產和負
   which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period, to          債賬面金額的方式所導致的納稅後果。
   recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and liabilities.

   Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there is a           倘有可依法執行權利以即期稅項資產抵
   legally enforceable right to set off current tax assets against          銷即期稅項負債，而遞延稅項與由同一
   current tax liabilities and when they relate to income taxes levied      稅務機構徵收的所得稅相關，且本集團
   by the same taxation authority and the Group intends to settle its       有意以淨額基準結算其即期稅項資產及
   current tax assets and liabilities on a net basis.                       負債，則遞延稅項資產及遞延稅項負債
                                                                            可相互抵銷。

   Current and deferred tax are recognised in profit or loss, except        即期和遞延稅項於損益中確認，除非其
   when they relate to items that are recognised in other                   與於其他全面收入確認或直接於權益確
   comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case, the           認的項目相關，在此情況下，即期和遞
   current and deferred tax are also recognised in other                    延稅項亦分別於其他全面收入確認或直
   comprehensive income or directly in equity respectively. Where           接於權益確認。倘業務合併初始會計列
   current tax or deferred tax arises from the initial accounting for a     賬產生即期稅項或遞延稅項，則稅項影
   business combination, the tax effect is included in the accounting       響計入業務合併的會計列賬。
   for the business combination.

   The effective interest method is a method of calculating the              實際利率法乃計算金融資產或金融負債
   amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of         攤銷成本及按有關期間攤分利息開支的
   allocating interest income and interest expense over the relevant         方法。實際利率乃於初步確認時將估計
   period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly              未來現金收入及付款（包括構成實際利率
   discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments (including          一部分的所有已付及已收費用、交易成
   all fees paid or received that form an integral part of the effective     本及其他溢價或折讓）按金融資產或金融
   interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or discounts)         負債的預期可使用年期，或（視乎情況而
   through the expected life of the financial asset or financial             定）較短期間準確貼現至賬面淨值的利
   liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net            率。
   carrying amount on initial recognition.

   (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                                   (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
        The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of              預期信貸虧損的計量依據為違約概
        default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if          率、違約損失率（即違約時的損失
        there is a default) and the exposure at default. The                    程度）及違約風險的函數。評估違
        assessment of the probability of default and loss given                 約概率及違約損失率的依據為過往
        default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-                數據，並按前瞻性資料調整。預期
        looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased             信貸虧損的估計反映以發生相關違
        and probability‐weighted amount that is determined with                 約風險的金額作為加權數值而確定
        the respective risks of default occurring as the weights.               的公允概率加權金額。

        Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference between             一般而言，預期信貸虧損按根據合
        all contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in               約應付本集團的所有合約現金流量
        accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the              與本集團預計收取的所有現金流量
        Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                 （按於初始確認時釐定的實際利率
        interest rate determined at initial recognition.                      折現）之間的差額作出估計。

        Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying             利息收入根據金融資產的賬面總值
        amount of the financial asset unless the financial asset is           計算，惟金融資產為信貸減值的情

        based on the amortised cost of the financial asset.                   據金融資產的攤銷成本計算。

        loss allowance is recognised in OCI and accumulated in the            入的貿易應收款項而言，虧損撥備

   Fair value measurement of land and buildings                           土地及樓宇的公平值計量
   Certain of the Group’s land and buildings are measured at fair         就財務申報而言，本集團部分土地及樓
   value for financial reporting purposes. The directors of the           宇按公平值計量。本公司董事負責確定
   Company are responsible for determining the appropriate                適當的估值技術及輸入數據以計量公平
   valuation techniques and inputs for fair value measurement.            值。

   In estimating the fair value of an asset, the Group uses market-       本集團使用可獲得的可觀察市場數據估
   observable data to the extent it is available. Where Level 1 inputs    計資產的公平值。倘並無第一級輸入數
   are not available, the Group engages third party qualified valuers     據，本集團會委聘第三方合資格估值師
   to perform the valuation. The directors of the Company work            進行估值。本公司董事與合資格外部估
   closely with the qualified external valuers to establish the           值師緊密合作，以建立模型適用的估值
   appropriate valuation techniques and inputs to the model.              技術及輸入數據。

   The Group uses valuation techniques that include inputs that are       本集團採用包括並非以可觀察市場數據
   not based on observable market data to estimate the fair value of      為依據的輸入數據在內的估值技術估計
   properties. Note 11 provides detailed information about the            物業的公平值。附註 11 載有有關釐定物
   valuation techniques, inputs and key assumptions used in the           業公平值所用的估值技術、輸入數據及
   determination of fair value of the properties.                         主要假設的詳細資料。

   or changes in circumstances indicate that their carrying amounts       告期末可能無法收回，本公司董事須進
   may not be recoverable at the end of the reporting period. An          行賬面值檢討。減值虧損按資產賬面值
   impairment loss is recognised for the amount by which an               超出其可收回金額的金額予以確認。可
   asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount. The            收回金額為資產公平值減出售成本及使
   recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less         用價值的較高者。
   costs of disposal and value in use.

   In determining whether an asset is impaired, the Group has to          於釐定資產是否減值時，本集團須作出
   exercise judgment and make estimation, particularly in assessing:      判斷及估計，尤其需要評估：(1) 是否發
   (1) whether an event has occurred or there are any indicators that     生可能影響資產價值的事件或任何跡象；
   may affect the asset value; (2) whether, in case there is an           (2) 倘存在減值跡象，資產的賬面值能否
   impairment indicator, the carrying value of an asset can be            以使用價值（即根據持續使用資產估計的
   supported by the recoverable amount, in the case of value in use,      未來現金流量之現值淨額）的可收回金額
   the net present value of future cash flows which are estimated         支持；及 (3) 估計可收回金額時所採用的
   based upon the continued use of the asset; and (3) the                 適當主要假設，包括現金流量預測及適
   appropriate key assumptions to be applied in estimating the            當貼現率。
   recoverable amounts including cash flow projections and an
   appropriate discount rate.

      20. TRADE RECEIVABLES AT FAIR VALUE                                     20. 按公平值計入其他全面收
          THROUGH OTHER COMPREHENSIVE INCOME                                      入的貿易應收款項
          As part of the Group’s cash flow management, the Group has           作為本集團現金流量管理的一部分，本
          the practice of factoring certain trade receivables to financial     集團的做法是在應收款項到期償還前，
          institutions before the receivables are due for repayment. The       向金融機構保理若干貿易應收款項。由
          factored trade receivables are derecognised on the basis that the    於本集團已將絕大部分風險及回報轉移
          Group has transferred substantially all the risks and rewards to     至相關交易對手，故已保理貿易應收款
          the relevant counterparties. Accordingly, such trade receivables     項已終止確認。因此，該等貿易應收款
          were under a business model which is held to collect contractual     項乃根據旨在收取合約現金流量及出售
          cash flows and to sell, and have been reclassified to trade          的業務模式持有，並分類為按公平值計
          receivables at FVTOCI.                                               入其他全面收入計量的貿易應收款項。

          At 31 December 2018, the effective interest rates of the             於二零一八年十二月三十一日，按公平
          factoring trade receivables at FVTOCI ranged from 2.77% to           值計入其他全面收入計量的保理貿易應
          2.90% per annum. Details of the valuation techniques and key         收 款 項 的 實 際 年 利 率 介 乎 2.77% 至
          inputs adopted for their fair value measurements are disclosed in    2.90%。有關公平值計量採用的估值技
          note 32c.                                                            術及主要輸入數據詳情於附註32c披露。

          Details of the impairment assessment of trade receivables at         有關截至二零一八年十二月三十一日止

   Investment risk                                                           投資風險
   The present value of the defined benefit plan liability is measured       定額福利計劃負債的現值採用預計單位
   by discounting the best estimate of future cash flows to be paid          給付成本法透過貼現計劃將予支付的未
   out by the plan using the projected unit credit method; if the            來現金流量的最佳估計計量；倘計劃資
   return on plan assets is below this rate, it will create a plan           產回報低於該利率，將出現計劃虧絀。
   deficit. Currently the plan has a relatively balanced portfolio in        目前，計劃的股權、多元化增長基金、
   equity investments, diversified growth funds, debt investments,           債務工具、負債驅動的投資基金及物業
   liability driven investment funds and a property fund. Due to the         基金組合較均衡。由於計劃負債屬長期
   long-term nature of the plan liabilities, the trustees of the pension     性質，退休金的受託人認為計劃資產投
   fund consider it appropriate that the plan assets should be               資於該組合屬恰當，以平衡基金產生的
   invested in the portfolio to leverage the return generated by the         回報。
   fund.

   Interest rate risk                                                        利率風險
   A decrease in the interest rate will increase the plan liability;         利率下降將導致計劃負債增加；然而，
   however, this will be partially offset by an increase in the return       這將被計劃的公司債券回報及負債驅動
   on the plan’s corporate bonds and liability driven investment             的投資基金增加而部分抵銷。
   funds.

   Inflation risk                                                            通脹風險
   The plan allows for rates of inflation experienced in the U.K. to         該計劃允許英國的通脹率適用於預期福
   apply to prospective benefits. The amounts of inflation                   利。通脹金額調整按計劃契約所載指數
   adjustments are calculated in accordance with indices set out in          計算。
   the plan deed.

   The present value of the defined benefit plan liability is calculated     定額福利計劃的現值參考有關計劃參與
   by reference to the best estimate of the mortality of plan                者死亡率的最佳估計計算。計劃參與者
   participants. An increase in the life expectancy of the plan              預期壽命增加將導致計劃負債增加。
   participants will increase the plan’s liability.

   Significant actuarial assumptions for the determination of the          釐定定額責任所用主要精算假設為貼現
   defined obligation are discount rate, inflation and mortality. The      率、通脹率及死亡率。以下敏感度分析
   sensitivity analyses below have been determined based on                乃基於各假設於報告期末的合理可能變
   reasonably possible changes of the respective assumptions               動，而所有其他假設不變而釐定。
   occurring at the end of the reporting period, while holding all
   other assumptions constant.

   •    If the discount rate decreases by 0.25%, the defined               •   如貼現率減少 0.25%，則定額福利
        benefit obligation would increase by 4.4% (2017: 4.1%).                責 任 將 增 加 4.4%（ 二 零 一 七 年：
                                                                               4.1%）。

        obligation would decrease by 4.1% (2017: 3.8%).                        責 任 將 減 少 4.1%（ 二 零 一 七 年：
                                                                               3.8%）。

        obligation would increase by 2.5% (2017: 2.5%).                        責 任 將 增 加 2.5%（ 二 零 一 七 年：
                                                                               2.5%）。

   •    If the life expectancy increases by one year for both men          •   如男性及女性預期壽命增加一年，
        and women, the defined benefit obligation would increase               則定額福利責任將增加 3.7%（二零
        by 3.7% (2017: 3.3%).                                                  一七年：3.3%）。

   The sensitivity analyses presented above may not be                     上文呈列的敏感度分析可能並不代表定
   representative of the actual change in the defined benefit              額福利責任的實際變動，因為若干假設
   obligation as it is unlikely that the change in assumptions would       可能互相關聯，致使假設不太可能獨立
   occur in isolation from one another as some of the assumptions          於其他假設出現變動。
   may be correlated.

   In presenting the above sensitivity analyses, the present value of      呈列上述敏感度分析時，定額福利責任
   the defined benefit obligation has been calculated using the            的現值已於報告期末採用預計單位給付
   projected unit credit method at the end of the reporting period,        成本法計算，計算於綜合財務狀況報表
   which is also applied in calculating the defined benefit obligation     確認的定額福利責任負債亦採用此方法。
   liability recognised in the consolidated statement of financial
   position.

30. SHARE-BASED PAYMENT TRANSACTIONS                                     30. 以股份為基礎的付款交易
   Share Award Scheme B                                                      股份獎勵計劃 B
   The Company adopted the share award scheme (“Share Award                  本公司根據二零一七年四月通過的一項
   Scheme B”) pursuant to a written resolution passed in April               書面決議案採納股份獎勵計劃（「股份獎
   2017, which approved the Company appointing an independent                勵計劃 B」），批准本公司委任一名獨立
   professional trustee (the “Trustee”) to assist with the                   專業受託人（「受託人」），以協助股份獎
   administration and vesting of the share awards. The purpose of            勵的管理和歸屬。股份獎勵計劃 B 的目
   Share Award Scheme B is to incentivise the Group’s executives,            的在於激勵本集團行政人員、顧問或高
   consultants or officers to contribute to the Group, to retain and         級職員為本集團作出貢獻，留住及激勵
   motivate skilled and experienced personnel and to incentivise             熟練、經驗豐富人員及透過向彼等提供
   them to strive for the future development of the Group by                 持有本公司股權的機會激勵彼等為本集
   providing them with the opportunity to own equity in the                  團的未來發展努力。
   Company.

   Share Award Scheme B is valid and effective for a period of ten           股份獎勵計劃 B 有效期為十年，自根據
   years, commencing from the date of the first grant of shares              該計劃首次授出股份當日開始。截至二
   under the scheme. During the year ended 31 December 2017,                 零 一 七 年 十 二 月 三 十 一 日 止 年 度，
   13,062,000 shares were granted to 93 persons eligible under               13,062,000 股股份已授予 93 名股份獎勵
   Share Award Scheme B. No consideration was paid by the                    計劃 B 下合資格人士。承授人並無就授
   grantees for the grant of the incentive shares. When the selected         出獎勵股份支付代價。當選定人士達到
   person has fulfilled the condition of service period specified in         股份獎勵計劃 B 所訂明服務期間的條件
   Share Award Scheme B, the Trustee shall transfer the relevant             時，受讓人將向該合資格人士轉讓相關
   incentive shares to that eligible person.                                 獎勵股份。

      31. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                             31. 資本風險管理
          The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the          本集團管理資本，確保本集團實體可持
          Group will be able to continue as going concerns while                續經營，同時透過審慎管理淨債務股本
          maximising the return to the shareholders through the careful         比率為股東創造最大回報。本集團的整
          management of the debt to equity ratio. The Group’s overall           體策略自過往年度起保持不變。
          strategy remains unchanged from prior years.

          The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which        本集團的資本結構由淨債務（包括附註
          includes the borrowings disclosed in note 26, less bank balances      26 披露的借款）、減銀行結餘及現金以及
          and cash and equity attributable to owners of the Company,            本公司擁有人應佔權益（包括已發行股
          comprising issued share capital, reserves and retained profits.       本、儲備及保留溢利）組成。

          The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a        本公司董事定期檢討資本架構。
          regular basis.
